10594 — resistance band lateral hit

Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 10594 after decompression) · Source record

Source completeness: needs_review. Storyboard proposal: proposal_ready_for_review. Pose class: standing.

A standing band press that sweeps across the body from the side. It builds chest power and trains the trunk to resist rotation.

Proposed staging

Starting Position

Anchor the band at chest height beside you and hold the handle at your chest. Stand tall in a stable stance with your core braced.

source_cue_reuse; source verified: true. 10594.json#/instructions/0, 10594.json#/instructions/1

Arms Hands

Let the upper arms rest near the torso, elbows soft and hands relaxed; allow small balance movements that do not add a new exercise action.

authored_scene_blocking; source verified: false. Scene support follows the source posture; unsupported inventory candidates are excluded from this proposal, not silently asserted. 10594.json#/instructions/1

Complete ordered source instructions

  1. Anchor the band at chest height beside you and hold the handle at your chest. 10594.json#/instructions/0
  2. Stand tall in a stable stance with your core braced. 10594.json#/instructions/1
  3. Press the handle forward and across your body in a quick controlled motion. 10594.json#/instructions/2
  4. Return slowly to your chest without letting the band pull you around. 10594.json#/instructions/3
  5. Keep your hips square throughout. 10594.json#/instructions/4
  6. Finish all reps, then switch sides. 10594.json#/instructions/5

10595 — medicine ball pullover catch

Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 10595 after decompression) · Source record

Source completeness: needs_review. Storyboard proposal: proposal_ready_for_review. Pose class: supine.

A lying throw and catch that sends a medicine ball overhead and back. It trains explosive lat and core strength.

Proposed staging

Starting Position

Lie on your back with knees bent, holding a medicine ball overhead.

source_cue_reuse; source verified: true. 10595.json#/instructions/0

Arms Hands

Rest the arms beside the torso with relaxed hands on the documented support surface; this is a balance/staging choice, not an added pressing action.

authored_from_source_posture; source verified: false. Initial arm position was not explicit; this is a neutral staging choice compatible with the inferred support pose. 10595.json#/instructions/0

authored_scene_blocking; source verified: false. Scene support follows the source posture; unsupported inventory candidates are excluded from this proposal, not silently asserted. 10595.json#/instructions/0

Complete ordered source instructions

  1. Lie on your back with knees bent, holding a medicine ball overhead. 10595.json#/instructions/0
  2. Pull the ball forward over your chest to throw it up toward a partner or wall. 10595.json#/instructions/1
  3. Catch the ball with soft elbows as it returns. 10595.json#/instructions/2
  4. Let it travel back overhead in a controlled arc. 10595.json#/instructions/3
  5. Keep your lower back pressed to the floor. 10595.json#/instructions/4
  6. Repeat at a steady rhythm. 10595.json#/instructions/5

10597 — cable 90 degrees internal rotation catch

Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 10597 after decompression) · Source record

Source completeness: needs_review. Storyboard proposal: proposal_ready_for_review. Pose class: standing.

A cable drill with the upper arm at shoulder height and elbow bent to 90 degrees. It trains the rotator cuff to catch and control a quick rotation.

Proposed staging

Starting Position

Set the cable at shoulder height and stand sideways to it.

source_cue_reuse; source verified: true. 10597.json#/instructions/0

Arms Hands

Let the upper arms rest near the torso, elbows soft and hands relaxed; allow small balance movements that do not add a new exercise action.

Complete ordered source instructions

  1. Set the cable at shoulder height and stand sideways to it. 10597.json#/instructions/0
  2. Raise your upper arm out to the side with the elbow bent 90 degrees. 10597.json#/instructions/1
  3. Let the cable pull your forearm back into external rotation. 10597.json#/instructions/2
  4. Catch it, then rotate the forearm forward and down smoothly. 10597.json#/instructions/3
  5. Keep your upper arm still and level. 10597.json#/instructions/4
  6. Complete the reps, then switch arms. 10597.json#/instructions/5

1060 — barbell hip thrust

Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 1060 after decompression) · Source record

Source completeness: needs_review. Storyboard proposal: proposal_ready_for_review. Pose class: supine.

A bench-supported bridge with a barbell across your hips. It is a go-to move for building strong glutes.

Proposed staging

Starting Position

Sit on the floor with your upper back against a bench and a padded barbell over your hips.

source_cue_reuse; source verified: true. 1060.json#/instructions/0

Arms Hands

Rest the arms beside the torso with relaxed hands on the documented support surface; this is a balance/staging choice, not an added pressing action.

authored_from_source_posture; source verified: false. Initial arm position was not explicit; this is a neutral staging choice compatible with the inferred support pose. 1060.json#/instructions/0

Legs Feet

For an unspecified unloaded face-up setup, bend the knees comfortably and ground the soles; preserve any source-defined straight-leg or elevated-leg action.

authored_from_source_posture; source verified: false. Initial leg placement was not explicit; this is a reversible balance/support choice, not a replacement for source movement. 1060.json#/instructions/0

Equipment Contacts

Retain every explicit contact in equipment_scene.contacts. Use only the documented prop choices: Straight Olympic barbell, Adjustable utility bench. Do not assign unknown apparatus dimensions, pad settings, attachment heights or load values.

authored_scene_blocking; source verified: false. Scene support follows the source posture; unsupported inventory candidates are excluded from this proposal, not silently asserted. 1060.json#/instructions/0

Complete ordered source instructions

  1. Sit on the floor with your upper back against a bench and a padded barbell over your hips. 1060.json#/instructions/0
  2. Plant your feet hip-width apart and drive through your heels. 1060.json#/instructions/1
  3. Lift your hips until your torso is level with the floor. 1060.json#/instructions/2
  4. Squeeze your glutes at the top and keep your chin tucked. 1060.json#/instructions/3
  5. Lower your hips under control. 1060.json#/instructions/4
  6. Repeat without arching your lower back. 1060.json#/instructions/5

A wide-stance squat with a barbell on your back. It hits the glutes and inner thighs along with the quads.

Proposed staging

Set the bar on your upper back and stand with feet well wider than your shoulders.
